The padding is gone. `r45_places.py` `HERO_P`, `r50_profile.py` `GUEST_P`.

## Words

- **Offline said one way (loop round 22):** "You’re offline. <What> needs a connection." everywhere a screen
  needs the network (Administration and Report a problem were "You are offline… requires an internet
  connection"); ru «Нет подключения к интернету. Для … нужна сеть.» (2026-11-15).
- **Professional wording, everywhere:** "refactor all the text and string and all names and write professionally, not
  plain words. write professional wording and sentences and not plain like you did before" — labels, settings, tips and
  empty states read like Apple, Chordify, Flat or MuseScore, in en and ru; this replaces the earlier "plain words" (2026-11-05).

- **The term a musician uses:** "check all the strings … use professional terms". Failed: Move smoothly, Between two chords,
  Starting position, Who wrote it, Through the keys, Hear notes as you edit, Tune, First chord.
- **Never a paraphrase, never an explanation line (canvas 158, 2026-11-11):** "why do you keep inventing bad and
  unprofessional names like in every key round the up by half steps and not chromatically or diatonically. check the
  contradicting instructions": Chromatically, Circle of fifths, Diatonically, Wait mode, Hands together, Inversions,
  Arpeggio, Alberti bass, Diatonic triads, Lead-sheet reading. Failed: In every key up by half steps, Round the circle,
  Through the key of, Waits for you, With the metronome, Missed chords come back, The four chords of a thousand songs,
  every "what it means in a line" under an option. The rules that invited it ("a name says what a control does",
  "familiar words over jargon", "plain questions", "one line on how it works") were rewritten or marked superseded.
- No ubiquitous-language glossary (2026-11-05): "i dont want the ubiquitous language anymore".

## Process
- **The lines tool reads true (2026-11-18):** a ruled table's rules are its drawn edge; a washed row's cousins count only when each row is wrapped alone (a See all link in a heading no longer made a grid of cards look like a current row); centred words have no left edge; h3 heads are measured.
- **A publish refused as "changed since you last saw it" is checked, never believed (owner, 2026-11-18: "fix the issues that you think that the artifact was changed since your last edit although it wasnt and you compare with the wrong state"):** the artifact service counts only what this conversation read, listed or published; after a compaction it has forgotten the earlier publishes, so my own last build reads as someone else's. At the start of a session and after every compaction, list the canvas's files once (`Artifact` `list`, `scope: "files"`): a listed path counts as seen. When a publish is still refused, compare the published sha256 with the last committed build (`git show HEAD:<board> | sha256sum`): the same means it is my own build, publish; different means someone else changed it: read it, merge, then publish.
- **Targets side by side stand 8 apart or join (laws, A phone is not a small laptop 4, 2026-11-18; the lines tool's
  new NOTE):** a song row's link and its ··· stood 4 apart (All songs, a playlist), as did a course row's in Made by
  you, whose fold chevron also overlapped its row link by 4 (a −8 margin); the phone Chord player's chord boxes 4
  apart. A row and its ··· now join (0 apart, one row), the chevron's margin is gone and the course row's left padding
  takes the 4 so its words stay on the lessons' edge; the phone chart's boxes stand 8 apart (the laptop's 6 is within
  a pointer's floor of 4). The editor's note strip and folded tool row are each one capsule and now say so
  (`role="toolbar"`, "The chosen note", "Tools").
- **A text button is 50, or 44 in a row (laws, similarity: near-miss heights; the design system as layers 2,
  2026-11-18):** the canvas drew its honey and quiet buttons at 44, 48, 50, 52 and 56, with 48 and 52 for the same
  job as 50 (a sheet's Save, an empty state's way out, a run's Start, a result's Practise mistakes), and the
  Metronome's Start at 52 beside its own 50 Tap tempo. `BTN` now has two heights, 50 for a page's or sheet's act and
  44 compact in a row, and snaps any other; the Metronome's Start and the lesson's dock are 50 by hand. The Player's
  end-of-song Practise bars, Play again and Next were 12-round rectangles (48 honey, 44 white with a shadow) and its
  pattern sheet's Show n a 44 rectangle: all are `BTN`'s pill now. The update toast, 16 round and 56 tall, is the
  toast's 14 and 52. The round Play (52) and the + (56) are other shapes and keep theirs. The lines tool gains a NOTE
  for targets side by side 2–7 apart (8, or one joined row).
- **Every field names its keyboard (laws, A phone is not a small laptop 5, 2026-11-18):** of the canvas's 50 kinds of
  field only the email fields and the editor's Bar carried a keyboard; searches, names, titles and passwords opened the
  plain keys. The kit's `KB` holds each kind once (search, title, text, name, username, email, person, url, code, and a
  password's current or new autocomplete), and every field names one: the search fields `type="search"` with the
  search key, a song's, folder's, group's, lesson's or course's name capitalised by words with Done, a description or
  tag by sentences, Your name and Username their autocomplete, Name or email and the invite link without capitals,
  the notes field and "Type DELETE" in capitals with no correction, Sign in's password current and Create account's
  and Settings' new ones new-password. `NAMEFIELD`, `NEWNAME`, Settings' `FIELD`, the account's `INP` and Practice's
  `INPUT` take the kind as a parameter, so no board patches a field. 62 boards change; none in how they look.
- **Patched helpers folded into the kit (laws, the design system as layers 1–2, 2026-11-18):** eight `.replace()` patches on a helper's output became the helper's own parameters: `DOCK(…, w=440 | 'auto')` for the centred laptop selection bars (All songs, Your lessons, Takes, the editor's format bar), `BSHEET(…, tall=True)` for Search and Chord type, `SEGC(…, fill=True)` for the Player's mode switch. Every board builds the same picture; Your lessons' bar centres by the same rule as the others. One-off patches with no sibling stay where they are.
- **Design in four phases (owner, 2026-11-17: "can you learn to work and design the ui in such phases as here", the mebis course "Grundlagen – Knowledge Base", 2. Softwareergonomie):** sketch (the flow and each page's parts, in the spec), wireframe (`WIRE=1` on the lines tool: grey boxes and bars at the device sizes), mockup (the look), prototype (every tap works), each held to the five E's and the laws before the next; written into `ui-and-mockups` (Building a screen, 4) and `redesigning-a-screen` (step 5). The lines tool now also reports a board that throws (the SongSettings board drew blank on a stray `pick`).
- **The Tuner fills the laptop (loop round 60, 2026-11-16):** the instrument is the page's one focus, but its laptop board drew a 560 gauge in a 1460 column; the gauge is 720 and the note 168, centred on the content column, with Reference pitch under it ("the empty space should be properly used").
- **Every board passes the layout check (loop round 60, 2026-11-16):** the treble clef's top and tail dot were cut by their staff frames on the exercise, Player-practice and results boards; each staff now has room above and below. The Tuner's −50 and +50 fit inside the gauge. The Take board's 14 bar buttons, 25 wide each, are one bar scrubber, a slider 44 high across the strip. The check itself now skips text ellipsised on purpose, measures a floating toolbar's centre against its own column (the content column, never the window with its sidebar, as ruled), and leaves out the empty spacer that keeps Play centred when it compares heights.
- **Spacing on the 4/8 scale everywhere (loop round 60, 2026-11-16):** "read more about layout rules and such things and all the paddings and spacings": researched Apple HIG Layout ("use consistent spacing", standard margins, readable width), Atlassian's spacing scale (0–8 inside small parts, 12–24 between components, 32–80 for layout) and the 8pt-grid rule that a group's inside is no larger than its outside. Every board's gaps 6/10/14 became 8/12/16 and paddings 10/14 became 12/16 (about 360 values). The sidebar's inset is now 12. A heading sits closer to its own content than to the section above (Settings' General, Sign in). `checks/rhythm_measure.js` flags a card padded more than its gap to sibling cards, and a heading nearer the content above than its own. The browser's own button padding, the status bar, the 2px title and subtitle stack and the 6px concentric inset of the tab bar and menus are kept on purpose.
- **Loop round 46, a States box names each state once:** a board that lists its own Playing (Intervals) showed Playing twice, its own and the one the States tool adds for any board that plays; the tool keeps the first of each name, and adds no Playing where the board names its own (Take's Playing from bar 4) (2026-11-16).
- **Design only; the canvas made consistent (2026-11-15):** "now you need to work only on the design and no app
  implementation at all. fix all the inconsistencies in the design. like the keyboard is inconsistent across the app,
  floating keyboard, the headers, the floating actions. the new floating actions should be on the right. and the
  settings are not correctly placed … all the states should be properly made like the chords page does in this box
  outside of the phone frame. when you think that you should refactor something … research for the official
  guidelines and professional designer resources": no app code until the owner says; one keyboard, one floating
  keyboard, one bar, one floating action (bottom right), settings in their homes, every board's states in the box
  beside its frame; each refactor cited from HIG, Material 3 or designers' resources.
- **The computer used properly (2026-11-15):** "all the ui should be properly made also on computer and the empty
  space should be properly used": no laptop board a phone column in an empty screen.
- **Design first, then build to it (2026-11-13):** "why are you improving the current app and not first make design
  and after that implement the design as close as possible. do like this": in a loop, each area is redrawn on the
  canvas and published before any change to the app; the app is then built to the drawing as closely as it can and
  checked against it at both sizes. Never a screen patched in the app with no drawing first.
- **No questions in a loop** (2026-11-13): "in the autonomous mode you don't ask questions to me, you work
  autonomously": a loop's report ends with what was decided, never a question.
- **No unintended shots of sign-in prompts, welcomes or tours** (2026-11-05): dismissed before every shot
  (`clearOnboarding`), shot only when needed or asked for (`ONBOARDING=1`).
- **Never a screenshot before the page has fully loaded** (2026-11-05): `scripts/settle.mjs` before every shot.
- **Mockups in the owner's frames:** the real iPhone 17 Pro (app, Safari, and the smaller iPhone) and the MacBook Air
  with a macOS Safari window; inspired by the example apps and sites the owner sends (2026-11-05).
- **A signed-in learner is never asked to sign in again** after an update (2026-11-05).

- **Mockups and questions:** whole-page mockups with a feedback box each worked.
- **Test every UI at least twice** (2026-11-05).
- **Impeccable twice on each page and each mockup,** as it is made, never only at the round's end: "each page and each
  mockup and not only at the end" (2026-11-05).
- **Plan before building:** generic descriptions led to wrong builds. "Did you read it every time you implement something."
- **"Why did you stop?"** three times: keep going until the list is empty. A message is not a stop.
- **The maximum principle:** "this spawns too many agents and eats up all the usage".
- **No tests in a loop** (2026-11-14): "dont run the tests, no test"; the build runs once at an area's end.
- **The references show what to add:** "didn't I add screenshots for you to add what we don't have".
- **Don't decide UI trade-offs silently:** "dont make such bad decisions for yourself".
- **No committed `.env`:** "WHY IS the .env commited to the git". That decision was the owner's.
- **Learners' data:** never read it.
- **Skills** come from their official source; superpowers is `obra/superpowers`.
- **Interrupting means wait:** take in the new message and continue from it.
- **Everything built, nothing left,** and documented in the docs (2026-11-05).
- **A command over 10 seconds runs in the background** (2026-11-05).
- **Context under about 450k tokens** (2026-11-05).
- **Supabase:** any database action is allowed, directly, with best practices (2026-11-05).
- **Real device sizes, researched:** "research also in the internet not only assume the browser gives you the right sizes"
  (2026-11-05).
- **Preserve the owner's edits** to these files when rewriting them (2026-11-05).
- **Latest words win;** keep the brief consistent when they do.
- **Spacing by ratio, researched with numbers (owner, 2026-11-18: "how much spacing should they have to be related and how much to differentciacte from other, but not too much spacing? … take more specific and precise instructions"):** `edge-lines/references/laws.md` gains "Spacing: how close groups things, how far separates them, how far is too far": Kubovy & Wagemans 1995's attraction function (α ≈ 8: a gap 1.25× the shortest still pulls 0.14, 1.5× 0.02, 2× nothing), Nielsen 2026 (ratio, not amount; "grouping collapse"), Refactoring UI (no two scale values within 25%; more space around a group than within), Atlassian's token levels, Material 3's list tokens. Seven rules follow: between ÷ within at least 1.5, aim 2; no more than about 3× (over 4× and 56 or more cuts a thing off); a heading's space above 1.5–3× its space below; outer ≥ inner; no two neighbouring steps for one job; levels 4–8 / 0–12 / 16–32 phone, 24–48 laptop / 48–64; spacing agrees with the lines and boxes. `lines.mjs` now notes a heading under 1.5× or over 4×.
- **One section gap, whatever the head holds (2026-11-18, the spacing laws' rule 6: same level, same gap):** on Learn and Profile a section whose head carried See all stood 40–41 below the last section, the others 32, because the link's 44 reach made the head row 8 taller. The link keeps its 44 reach with `margin: -12px 0`, so every head is 32 above and 12 below (2.7×, inside the 1.5–3× band) on both sizes (`SEEALLH`, `SEEALL`, `SEEALL9`, `SHELF`).
- **Songs' sections 32 apart, as Learn's and Profile's (2026-11-18, spacing laws rule 6):** the Songs overview stood its sections 28 apart and All songs' head 28 above and 22 below its shelf (1.27×, the ambiguous band, because See all's 44 reach grew the head row): now 32 above and 12 below (2.7×), and a shared playlist's laptop page 32 too (`r72` `SEEALL`, the overview's and `shlm`'s gaps).
- **A lesson's example is named on its keys (2026-11-18, spacing laws rules 1 and 3):** the example's name (C major, with ⓘ and Play) stood about 33 under the paragraph and 26 over its keys, 1.27×, so it read as much the paragraph's end as the keys' title (Nielsen: "roughly half guess wrong"). The figure stands 8 further from the text and its head row reaches 8 into the gap below: about 40 above and 15 to the keys, 2.7×, on both sizes and on a question's figure too; sections 32 apart, as every page (`r47_edit` `DOC`).
- **The Uxcel lessons as exact rules (owner, 2026-11-18, the "What is UX Design?", "What is UX Writing?", "Basic Components of IA" and "How to Design Landing Pages That Convert" lessons with their do/don't screenshots: "add this to our rules and guidlines skills … not summaries but exact instructions and precise instructions"):** `edge-lines/references/laws.md` gains "Information architecture" (15 numbered checks: the three pillars written per page; collections over about 12 grouped by the key the learner hunts by, a top row of groups with All first; labels plain, of one kind, one per thing; the main navigation only the places; Search with help before typing; menus of groups, at most about seven, the main act at a menu's foot; the journey before the look) and "One outcome per screen" (14 checks: one named outcome; no second offer; one picture per block; paragraphs of at most 4 lines; titles of about 6 words; the one honey act 44+ high, on the first screen at 402×874 and 1710×904; a cue at the fold; real pictures; true social proof only; nothing floating over a phone's content but the bars and the one act). `ux-writing` gains six checks (every string has a job; no marketing on a working screen; fix the flow before the words; lengths for buttons, menu items, heads and toasts; words that disappear; one voice). `ui-and-mockups`' four phases now name them. The lines tool measures heading gaps ink to ink, and a head that opens its section against what stands before the section.
- **The song page's laptop sections 20 apart (2026-11-18, spacing laws rule 2):** its heads, whose kit `H2` adds 16 above, stood 56 above their content and 4–6 below, ink to ink, 9–14×, past the 3× where space only cuts a section off; the column's gap is 20, as the phone's 16 plus the head's own.
- **The landing-page screenshots' pairs, as checks (owner, 2026-11-18, the scroll cue, contextual image, social proof, brand authority and perks screenshots):** `laws.md` "One outcome per screen" 10–12a now carry each pair: a quiet ⌄ under the act where nothing peeks at the fold; the picture proves the headline (a song's cover is its own first bars, never a generic piano photo); proof is a named person's words, a real count with what it counts, or a known name, never an adjective over floating faces; a reward names the concrete thing it gives.
- **Which component for which job, as checks (owner, 2026-11-18, Uxcel "Common UI Components Part I"):** `laws.md` gains 13 numbered rules quoting the lesson: a button is a verb that acts now; checkboxes for several picks (empty ring, filled check); a radio group or segmented control for one of several, "None" as its own option; a switch only for an instant on/off; fields typed by kind with a visible label; menus from the control touched, never of one item; forms that ask only what the act needs; modals only from the learner's tap or for a loss that cannot be undone; cards as things you open; tables with a header for every column; one bar on every screen; a web footer's links at the foot of Settings.
- **Profile's streak names its reward (2026-11-18, "One outcome per screen" 12a):** "Today counts. Practise tomorrow to keep it going." is "Today counts. 2 more days to the Gold badge.", the true next step the badges row already shows.
- **Predictions and suggestions, as checks (owner, 2026-11-18, Uxcel "What Is AI-Powered UX?"):** `laws.md` gains 8 rules for where the app guesses (detected chords, suggested songs and lessons, Continue): a guess is a suggestion with accept, change and dismiss, never a decision; one suggestion block per screen, below the learner's content; an uncertain detection is marked and reviewed first; correcting is editing; a personal order says why and can be changed or cleared; the frame never rearranges by use; what is learned from playing can be seen, cleared and exported; where a rule gives the answer, it is shown as fact.
- **Cognitive biases, as checks (owner, 2026-11-18, Uxcel "Cognitive Biases"):** `laws.md` gains 11 rules: every shortcut or gesture also a visible control; unruled choices checked against two shipped apps; the first-run screens and Search checked first each round; no account before the first song; fixes checked by a measure that could prove them wrong, and a shot's date checked before judging it (a stale song-page shot misled a round today); errors as careful as the best moment; standard places for Search, Back and Settings; usual defaults shown as defaults; waits over 2 seconds say how long; progress framed as done and next; losses named truly.
- **More component rules (owner, 2026-11-18, Uxcel "Common UI Components", Design Terminology):** `laws.md` rules 14–24: a button changes something and a link goes somewhere, never swapped; one identical places bar; no hamburger; a trail's last step is plain ink, not a link; containers only for sections, rigid for pictures, flexible for words; menus of short commands, icons all or none, the loss last in crimson; segmented controls of 2–4 equal options; labels outside fields, placeholders only as examples; static labels never tappable; Off or None as its own radio; the link colour only on links.
- **Import reads before it shows (2026-11-18, laws "Cognitive biases" 9: a wait over about 2 seconds says how far it has got):** Choose a file jumped straight to the result; a 138-bar score takes seconds. Import now shows a reading step first: the file's cover, "Reading Fantaisie-Impromptu.mxl", "Bar 84 of 138 · about 2 seconds left" in tabular digits and an ink progress bar (`role=status`), then the result with Open song, on Songs, All songs and the playlist boards, phone and laptop (`r72` `IMPFORM`).
- **Canvas v541 (2026-11-18, loop): ProgressionEdit's sections stand 24 apart, 8 inside.** The lines tool noted "Lines"
  at 16 above, 12 below (1.33×, under the 1.5× floor; laws, Spacing); the edit column's sections now sit 24 apart, so
  every heading is 3× nearer what it heads. Phone and laptop. Search's first-open state was checked against biases
  rule 3: it opens on recent searches and suggestions, never a blank.
- **Canvas (2026-11-18, loop): Learn's and Songs' columns end clear of the +.** Scrolled to the end, the + covered the
  last row's chevron on Learn ("Notes from my teacher", 38px under it) and the last Explore card's names on Songs
  (46px). Both columns now end 176 above the screen's foot; a check over every phone board with a + found no other.
  The rule is written beside "Floating things float" in `ui-and-mockups`.
- **Rules (2026-11-18): Uxcel's "Characters in Typography" and "Designing for Mobile Interfaces"** written into
  `edge-lines/references/laws.md` as numbered checks: Type anatomy (18: the 12px floor, Literata never under 16 or in
  a sentence, gaps measured from the ink, two-line titles no tighter than 1.1, three tracking values, four weights,
  tabular for still numbers and columns, proportional in sentences, ligatures) and A phone is not a small laptop (8:
  portrait first and every feature on its side, sessions resume, thumb's reach, 8 between targets, the keyboard per
  field, a visible twin for every gesture, SVG and rem, big decisions complete on a phone). `seven-questions` 3 points
  to them.
- **Canvas (2026-11-18, loop): ProfileLaptop's rows meet.** "Last 7 days" stood 8 lower than "Badges" beside it: its
  caption ("152 min · 6 of 7 days") kept a 44 minimum height though it is no target. It takes its text's height now,
  and the rows stand 32 apart, not 48 (laws, Spacing: past about 3× the space cuts a section off).
- **Rules (2026-11-18): Uxcel's "Intro to Design Composition" and "What is a Design System?"** written into
  `edge-lines/references/laws.md`: Composition (7: format, grid, groups, then the look; one shared grid; the act after
  the facts it depends on, never over the picture; one focal point by scale; nothing random; one frame across
  sibling pages) and The design system, as layers (our four layers mapped, canvas and app; values from the layer
  below; a component once, in the kit; one name both sides; a docstring per helper; governance as the log; a core
  change checked on every board).
- **Canvas v543 (2026-11-18, loop): the type rules applied on every board.** A sweep of every phone board against
  laws, Type anatomy found text under 12px (the tab bar's labels at 11, the docks' captions Style · Key · Tempo · Tap ·
  Listen · From, key letters at 10, avatar initials and unread counts at 10), an inactive tab at weight 550, and two
  extra tracking values (-0.02em on tool titles, +0.06em on small capitals). All are 12px now, the inactive tab 500,
  titles -0.01em, small capitals +0.04em, in the kit and 20 generators (62 boards). The song cover's miniature
  printed title stays, being the picture. Rule 13 was corrected to the boards: 700 sets names, titles and the active
